class="mailing-list-message mailing-list-message--root"><header class="mailing-list-message__header"><span class="mailing-list-message__author">changmao wang</span><time datetime="2015-08-18T18:02:08-07:00">August 18, 2015 at 6:02 PM</time></header><div class="mailing-list-message__body">Matthew,

I used s3cmd --configure to generate &quot;.s3cfg&quot; config file and then access
RIAK service by s3cmd.
The access_key and secret_key from &quot;.s3cfg&quot; is same as admin_key
and admin_secret from &quot;/etc/riak-cs/app.config&quot;.

However, I got error as below using s3cmd to access one bucket.

root@cluster-s3-hd1:~# s3cmd -c /root/.s3cfg ls
s3://pipeline/article/111.pdf
ERROR: Access to bucket &#x27;pipeline&#x27; was denied

By the way, I used Riak and Riak-CS 1.4.2 on Ubuntu. Current production
cluster is a legacy system without documents for co-workers.

Attached file is &quot;s3cfg&quot; generated by &quot;s3cmd --configure&quot;.
--
Amao Wang
Best &amp; Regards

.s3cfg

Description: Binary data

If so, there&#x27;re same as &quot;api2.cloud-datayes.com&quot;. However, I can not ping
this host from localhost.

I got such error in this case.

s3://pipeline/article/111.pdf

riak-users mailing list
riak-users@lists.basho.com
<a href="http://lists.basho.com/mailman/listinfo/riak-users_lists.basho.com">http://lists.basho.com/mailman/listinfo/riak-users_lists.basho.com</a>
</div><ol class="mailing-list-replies"><li class="mailing-list-reply"><details id="msg16442" class="mailing-list-message"><summary><header class="mailing-list-message__header"><span class="mailing-list-message__author">Kazuhiro Suzuki</span><time datetime="2015-08-20T07:32:40-07:00">August 20, 2015 at 7:32 AM</time><span class="mailing-list-message__subject">Re: s3cmd error: access to bucket was denied</span></header></summary><div class="mailing-list-message__content"><div class="mailing-list-message__body">Hi Changmao,

It seems your s3cmd config should include 2 items:

signature_v2 = True
host_base  = api2.cloud-datayes.com

Riak CS requires &quot;signature_v2 = True&quot; since Riak CS has not supported
s3 authentication version 4 yet.
You can find a sample configuration of s3cmd here to interact with Riak CS [1].

[1]:
<a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>

Thanks,

On Thu, Aug 20, 2015 at 7:44 PM, changmao wang &lt;wang.chang...@gmail.com&gt; wrote:
<blockquote>Just now, I used &quot;admin_key&quot; and &quot;admin_secret&quot; from /etc/riak-cs/app.config
to run &quot;s3cmd -c s3-stock ls  s3://stock/XSHE/0/000600&quot;
and I got the below error:

</div><ol class="mailing-list-replies"><li class="mailing-list-reply"><details id="msg16445" class="mailing-list-message"><summary><header class="mailing-list-message__header"><span class="mailing-list-message__author">changmao wang</span><time datetime="2015-08-20T18:01:06-07:00">August 20, 2015 at 6:01 PM</time><span class="mailing-list-message__subject">Re: s3cmd error: access to bucket was denied</span></header></summary><div class="mailing-list-message__content"><div class="mailing-list-message__body">Kazuhiro,

Maybe that&#x27;s not the key point. I&#x27;m using riak 1.4.2 and follow below docs
to configure &quot;s3cfg&quot; file.
<a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/1.4.2/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/1.4.2/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>

There&#x27;s no &quot;signature_v2&quot; parameter in &quot;s3cfg&quot;. However, I added this
parameter to &quot;s3cfg&quot; and tried again with same errors.

<blockquote>Hi Changmao,

It seems your s3cmd config should include 2 items:

Riak CS requires &quot;signature_v2 = True&quot; since Riak CS has not supported

</div></div></details></li><li class="mailing-list-reply"><details id="msg16446" class="mailing-list-message"><summary><header class="mailing-list-message__header"><span class="mailing-list-message__author">Shunichi Shinohara</span><time datetime="2015-08-23T18:06:02-07:00">August 23, 2015 at 6:06 PM</time><span class="mailing-list-message__subject">Re: s3cmd error: access to bucket was denied</span></header></summary><div class="mailing-list-message__content"><div class="mailing-list-message__body">The error message in console.log shows no user with access_key in your s3cfg.
Could you provide resutls following commands?

- s3cmd ls
- s3cmd info s3://stock

If error happens, debug print switch &quot;-d&quot; of s3cmd might help.

<blockquote>Kazuhiro,

</blockquote>docs
<blockquote>to configure &quot;s3cfg&quot; file.

</blockquote><a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/1.4.2/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/1.4.2/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>
<blockquote>
There&#x27;s no &quot;signature_v2&quot; parameter in &quot;s3cfg&quot;. However, I added this
parameter to &quot;s3cfg&quot; and tried again with same errors.

On Thu, Aug 20, 2015 at 10:31 PM, Kazuhiro Suzuki &lt;k...@basho.com&gt; wrote:
<blockquote>
Hi Changmao,

It seems your s3cmd config should include 2 items:

signature_v2 = True
host_base  = api2.cloud-datayes.com

Riak CS requires &quot;signature_v2 = True&quot; since Riak CS has not supported
s3 authentication version 4 yet.
You can find a sample configuration of s3cmd here to interact with Riak
</blockquote></blockquote>CS
<blockquote><blockquote>[1].

[1]:

</blockquote></blockquote><a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>
<blockquote><blockquote>

is no bucket with name &quot;stock&quot;:

<blockquote>root@cluster-s3-hd1:~# s3cmd ls
2013-12-01 06:45  s3://test
</blockquote>
Have you created it?

--
Shunichi Shinohara
Basho Japan KK

<blockquote>Kazuhiro,

Maybe that&#x27;s not the key point. I&#x27;m using riak 1.4.2 and follow below
docs
to configure &quot;s3cfg&quot; file.

<a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/1.4.2/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/1.4.2/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>

There&#x27;s no &quot;signature_v2&quot; parameter in &quot;s3cfg&quot;. However, I added this
parameter to &quot;s3cfg&quot; and tried again with same errors.

On Thu, Aug 20, 2015 at 10:31 PM, Kazuhiro Suzuki &lt;k...@basho.com&gt; wrote:
<blockquote>
Hi Changmao,

It seems your s3cmd config should include 2 items:

signature_v2 = True
host_base  = api2.cloud-datayes.com

Riak CS requires &quot;signature_v2 = True&quot; since Riak CS has not supported
s3 authentication version 4 yet.
You can find a sample configuration of s3cmd here to interact with Riak
CS
[1].

[1]:

<a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>

Thanks,

</div></div></details></li><li class="mailing-list-reply"><details id="msg16449" class="mailing-list-message"><summary><header class="mailing-list-message__header"><span class="mailing-list-message__author">Shunichi Shinohara</span><time datetime="2015-08-23T18:55:39-07:00">August 23, 2015 at 6:55 PM</time><span class="mailing-list-message__subject">Re: s3cmd error: access to bucket was denied</span></header></summary><div class="mailing-list-message__content"><div class="mailing-list-message__body">What is &quot;api2.cloud-datayes.com&quot;? Your s3cfg attached at the first one
in this email thread
does not include it. Please make sure you provide correct / consistent
information to
debug the issue.

- What is your riak cs config &quot;cs_root_host&quot;?
- What is your host_base in s3cfg that you USE?
- What is your host_bucket in s3cfg?

Also, please attach s3cmd debug output AND riak cs console log at the same time
interval.
--
Shunichi Shinohara
Basho Japan KK

On Thu, Aug 20, 2015 at 10:31 PM, Kazuhiro Suzuki &lt;k...@basho.com&gt;
wrote:
<blockquote>
Hi Changmao,

It seems your s3cmd config should include 2 items:

signature_v2 = True
host_base  = api2.cloud-datayes.com

Riak CS requires &quot;signature_v2 = True&quot; since Riak CS has not
supported
s3 authentication version 4 yet.
You can find a sample configuration of s3cmd here to interact with
Riak
CS
[1].

[1]:

<a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>

Thanks,

On Thu, Aug 20, 2015 at 10:31 PM, Kazuhiro Suzuki &lt;k...@basho.com&gt;
wrote:
<blockquote>
Hi Changmao,

It seems your s3cmd config should include 2 items:

signature_v2 = True
host_base  = api2.cloud-datayes.com

Riak CS requires &quot;signature_v2 = True&quot; since Riak CS has not
supported
s3 authentication version 4 yet.
You can find a sample configuration of s3cmd here to interact
with
Riak
CS
[1].

[1]:

<a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>

Thanks,

production
cluster is a legacy system without documents for
co-workers.

--configure&quot;.

There&#x27;s no &quot;signature_v2&quot; parameter in &quot;s3cfg&quot;. However, I added
</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote>this
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>parameter to &quot;s3cfg&quot; and tried again with same errors.

On Thu, Aug 20, 2015 at 10:31 PM, Kazuhiro Suzuki &lt;k...@basho.com&gt;
wrote:
<blockquote>
Hi Changmao,

It seems your s3cmd config should include 2 items:

signature_v2 = True
host_base  = api2.cloud-datayes.com

Riak CS requires &quot;signature_v2 = True&quot; since Riak CS has not
supported
s3 authentication version 4 yet.
You can find a sample configuration of s3cmd here to interact with
Riak
CS
[1].

[1]:

</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote><a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>
Thanks,

wrote:
<blockquote>
2015-08-20 14:47 GMT+05:00 changmao wang

<blockquote>what&#x27;s your meaning of domain name of /etc/riak-cs/app.config
and
~/.s3cfg?
I guess it&#x27;s cs_root_host parameter from
/etc/riak-cs/app.config
and
host_base from &#x27;~/.s3cfg&#x27;.
If so, there&#x27;re same as &quot;api2.cloud-datayes.com&quot;.
</blockquote>
Yes, is that i mean, but i see, it is not your case
Try to set {level, debug} in lager_file_backend section for
console.log.

<blockquote>However, I can not ping this host from localhost.
</blockquote>
It&#x27;s ok, if you write proper proxy_host and proxy_port in
</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote>.s3cfg
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>
<blockquote>On Thu, Aug 20, 2015 at 5:23 PM, Stanislav Vlasov
&lt;stanislav....@gmail.com&gt;
wrote:
<blockquote>
2015-08-20 13:57 GMT+05:00 changmao wang
&lt;wang.chang...@gmail.com&gt;:
<blockquote>somebody watching on this?
</blockquote>

I got such error in this case.

<blockquote>On Wed, Aug 19, 2015 at 9:01 AM, changmao wang
&lt;wang.chang...@gmail.com&gt;
wrote:
<blockquote>
Matthew,

I used s3cmd --configure to generate &quot;.s3cfg&quot; config file
and
then
access
RIAK service by s3cmd.
The access_key and secret_key from &quot;.s3cfg&quot; is same as
admin_key
and
admin_secret from &quot;/etc/riak-cs/app.config&quot;.

However, I got error as below using s3cmd to access one
bucket.

root@cluster-s3-hd1:~# s3cmd -c /root/.s3cfg ls
s3://pipeline/article/111.pdf
ERROR: Access to bucket &#x27;pipeline&#x27; was denied

By the way, I used Riak and Riak-CS 1.4.2 on Ubuntu.
Current
production
cluster is a legacy system without documents for
co-workers.

Attached file is &quot;s3cfg&quot; generated by &quot;s3cmd
</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote>--configure&quot;.

<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>

On Thu, Aug 20, 2015 at 10:31 PM, Kazuhiro Suzuki &lt;k...@basho.com&gt;
</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote>wrote:
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>
Hi Changmao,

It seems your s3cmd config should include 2 items:

signature_v2 = True
host_base  = api2.cloud-datayes.com

Riak CS requires &quot;signature_v2 = True&quot; since Riak CS has not
</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote>supported
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>s3 authentication version 4 yet.
You can find a sample configuration of s3cmd here to interact with
</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote>Riak
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>CS
[1].

[1]:

</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote><a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>
Thanks,

</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote><a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/1.4.2/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/1.4.2/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>
There&#x27;s no &quot;signature_v2&quot; parameter in &quot;s3cfg&quot;. However, I added
this
parameter to &quot;s3cfg&quot; and tried again with same errors.

On Thu, Aug 20, 2015 at 10:31 PM, Kazuhiro Suzuki &lt;
</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote>k...@basho.com&gt;
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>wrote:
<blockquote>
Hi Changmao,

It seems your s3cmd config should include 2 items:

signature_v2 = True
host_base  = api2.cloud-datayes.com

Riak CS requires &quot;signature_v2 = True&quot; since Riak CS has not
supported
s3 authentication version 4 yet.
You can find a sample configuration of s3cmd here to interact
with
Riak
CS
[1].

[1]:

</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote><a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>
Thanks,

On Thu, Aug 20, 2015 at 10:31 PM, Kazuhiro Suzuki &lt;
</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote>k...@basho.com&gt;
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>wrote:
<blockquote>
Hi Changmao,

It seems your s3cmd config should include 2 items:

signature_v2 = True
host_base  = api2.cloud-datayes.com

Riak CS requires &quot;signature_v2 = True&quot; since Riak CS has not
supported
s3 authentication version 4 yet.
You can find a sample configuration of s3cmd here to interact
with
Riak
CS
[1].

[1]:

</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote><a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>
Thanks,

</div></div></details></li><li class="mailing-list-reply"><details id="msg16461" class="mailing-list-message"><summary><header class="mailing-list-message__header"><span class="mailing-list-message__author">Stanislav Vlasov</span><time datetime="2015-08-25T22:06:02-07:00">August 25, 2015 at 10:06 PM</time><span class="mailing-list-message__subject">Re: s3cmd error: access to bucket was denied</span></header></summary><div class="mailing-list-message__content"><div class="mailing-list-message__body">2015-08-25 11:03 GMT+05:00 changmao wang &lt;wang.chang...@gmail.com&gt;:
<blockquote>Any ideas on this issue?
</blockquote>

s3curl, for example?

I got some bugs in s3cmd after debian upgrade, so if another client
works, than s3cmd has bug.

<blockquote>On Mon, Aug 24, 2015 at 5:09 PM, changmao wang &lt;wang.chang...@gmail.com&gt;
wrote:

On Thu, Aug 20, 2015 at 10:31 PM, Kazuhiro Suzuki
&lt;k...@basho.com&gt;
wrote:
<blockquote>
Hi Changmao,

It seems your s3cmd config should include 2 items:

signature_v2 = True
host_base  = api2.cloud-datayes.com

Riak CS requires &quot;signature_v2 = True&quot; since Riak CS has not
supported
s3 authentication version 4 yet.
You can find a sample configuration of s3cmd here to interact
with
Riak
CS
[1].

[1]:

<a href="http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use">http://docs.basho.com/riakcs/2.0.1/cookbooks/configuration/Configuring-an-S3-Client/#Sample-s3cmd-Configuration-File-for-Production-Use</a>

Thanks,

</div></div></details></li><li class="mailing-list-reply"><details id="msg16462" class="mailing-list-message"><summary><header class="mailing-list-message__header"><span class="mailing-list-message__author">Shunichi Shinohara</span><time datetime="2015-08-26T02:37:08-07:00">August 26, 2015 at 2:37 AM</time><span class="mailing-list-message__subject">Re: s3cmd error: access to bucket was denied</span></header></summary><div class="mailing-list-message__content"><div class="mailing-list-message__body">Sorry for delay and thanks for further information.
The log of Riak CS shows timeout in list query to Riak:
   2015-08-24 16:59:49.717 [error] &lt;0.18027.7&gt; gen_fsm &lt;0.18027.7&gt; in state
       waiting_list_keys terminated with reason: &lt;&lt;&quot;timeout&quot;&gt;&gt;

There are some kinds of possibility for timeout, e.g. hardware resource.
But if the timeout is occured by large number of objects (manifests) in your
bucket, improvement from 1.4.0 may help.
It can be used by setting
   {fold_objects_for_list_keys, true}
in your app.config&#x27;s riak_cs section or executing
   application:set_env(riak_cs, fold_objects_for_list_keys, true).
by attaching to riak-cs node.
For more information about it, please refer the original PR [1].

[1] <a href="https://github.com/basho/riak_cs/pull/600">https://github.com/basho/riak_cs/pull/600</a>
--
Shunichi Shinohara
Basho Japan KK

On Wed, Aug 26, 2015 at 2:04 PM, Stanislav Vlasov

</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote>not
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>supported

</blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote></blockquote>interact
<blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote><blockquote>with
